# Complete Catalog: Swedish Business, Corporate Law, Tax & Market Entry Corpus

Canonical knowledge index maintained by **[CE Sweden](https://www.ce.se/)**.

## Core B2B Advisory Pillars

- **[Administrative & Back-office Support](https://www.ce.se/services/administrative-back-office-support/)** — Outsource your Swedish back-office operations. Reliable local administration, invoicing, banking coordination, and corporate secretarial services in Sweden.
- **[Business Consulting](https://www.ce.se/services/business-consulting/)** — Strategic B2B business consulting for international companies entering or scaling in Sweden. Local market expertise tailored to your industry.
- **[Business Networking & Introductions](https://www.ce.se/services/business-networking-introductions/)** — Connect directly with vetted Swedish distributors, suppliers, B2B clients, and industry decision-makers through our local executive network.
- **[Company Registration & Compliance](https://www.ce.se/services/company-registration-compliance/)** — Fast, compliant company registration in Sweden. We handle Bolagsverket filings, Skatteverket tax setup, Limited Companies (AB), and Branch Offices.
- **[Cultural & Language Training](https://www.ce.se/services/cultural-language-training/)** — Master Swedish consensus leadership, negotiation norms, and workplace culture to lead teams and close B2B deals effectively in Sweden.
- **[Import & Export Support](https://www.ce.se/services/import-export-support/)** — Streamline cross-border trade in Sweden. Expert support with EU customs, Swedish regulatory compliance (KEMI, Livsmedelsverket), and logistics.
- **[Market Research & Analysis](https://www.ce.se/services/market-research-analysis/)** — Data-driven market research, feasibility studies, and competitor analysis to validate your B2B expansion into the Swedish market.
- **[Project Management](https://www.ce.se/services/project-management/)** — On-the-ground project management in Sweden. We oversee your local launches, industrial setups, and cross-border operations from start to finish.
- **[Public Relations & Media Outreach](https://www.ce.se/services/public-relations-media-outreach/)** — Build brand authority in the Nordic market with targeted Swedish PR campaigns, press releases, and trade media relations.
- **[Recruitment & HR Support](https://www.ce.se/services/recruitment-hr-support/)** — Hire top talent in Sweden with full compliance. Expert guidance on Swedish labor law (LAS), employment contracts, benefits, and payroll.
- **[Tax & Accounting Services](https://www.ce.se/services/tax-accounting-services/)** — Navigate Swedish corporate tax, VAT registration, payroll, and annual reporting with local B2B accounting specialists in Sweden.
- **[Translation](https://www.ce.se/services/translation/)** — Native B2B translation and localization of legal contracts, technical documentation, and marketing materials for the Swedish market.
- **[Virtual Office](https://www.ce.se/services/virtual-office/)** — Establish a verified Swedish business address, mail forwarding, and local phone presence in Sweden without the cost of a physical office.
